# A-01. Source ledger and permitted use

Revision 12 · Stage 2, response 12 · 2026-11-14

This is the full ledger for material actually cited in the current chapters or experiment cards. The working record contains a compact per-source/version/use/licence index. Source IDs are stable; unused rows have been removed rather than presented as support. The input inventory in the plan separately records files read, including reviewed concept reports not currently cited by a chapter.

**Authority:** user corrections, then the one-pager's scope, compatible charter elaboration and supplied CalcVBA facts/decisions, with the assignment governing the work. Reviewed reports contribute concepts only. Public references support documentary facts and design concepts; they do not become actual executions of ACE 16.0.20527.20016. No ACE experiment has been run here.

**Reuse boundary:** no proprietary decompilation/disassembly; no direct FrankenSqlite/asupersync repositories, mirrors, packages or external source extracts. The nine user-cleared first-stage reports use the canonical filenames below. Jackcess exact source headers/material are distinguished from project-wide headlines and uninspected resources; mdbtools remains file-format-facts only. No source code, test suite or resource has been ported in this book. A future port requires the exact pinned artifact, applicable licence/notices and attribution before reuse.

A live page with an access date is not an immutable source revision. An unresolved commit/release or material licence stays explicitly unresolved. “Not separately checked” permits the stated factual paraphrase, not adaptation or source reuse. No long verbatim extract is used. Citations in the final column identify substantive chapters/cards rather than this ledger itself.
